Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in modular theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 281 and the invariant I(A,B) = 312 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 281 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the modular structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 312. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
